# Farghānī — CUMULATIVE SELECTED bilingual authorial checks · Stage XVI (9 November 2026)

**559 complete source-graded *selected excerpt records*** from B2007 printed page labels 18–145, updated from original-resolution 1876 printed photographs with both p48 *subuḥāt* and p52 *al-Ḥakam* corrections. **This is NOT a full-page Arabic transcription, not a continuous established Arabic critical edition, and not a proofread manuscript witness.**

**Record quality:** 283 checked against modern edited/digitized Beirut Arabic selections on pp18–60, and 276 from a *secondary, derivative* online retranscription on pp61–145; do not count the latter as a separate printed or handwritten witness. Direct older print evidence: **15 bounded photographic I1876-versus-modern-digital/derivative loci** (7 differences, 8 agreements), on 14 historical printed page labels; ZERO entire historical printed pages collated, ZERO medieval MS folios read, ZERO MS variants. Every B2007 introductory printed page has at least one selected entry but no complete critical Arabic proof.


**New Stage XV corrections:** I1876 printed p20 scan24 corroborates `القابل` at B2007 p38, contrary to earlier guess `القائل`. I1876 printed p28 scan32 reads `بسبحات`, NOT modern digital `بسبحاب`; the provisional edited lemma translates **'splendours/radiances'**, not earlier 'clouds'. I1876 printed p38 scan42 confirms a bounded al-Jawād gift sequence, and p39 scan43 confirms the al-Ghaffār/al-Ghafūr distinction. See [photographic evidence report](I1876_COLLATION_STAGE_015.md) and [new 15-locus apparatus](I1876_COLLATION_CUMULATIVE_006.json).


**Stage XVI additional verified result:** Original-resolution photographed I1876 printed p32 reads **اسم الحكم** (the Judge) where the modern digitized B2007 source for printed p52 reads **سم الحكيم** (the Wise). Corrected p52 translation and updated the witness-specific apparatus. [Source photo](../facsimile-I1876-hires/images/scan_0036.jpg) · [philological report](I1876_COLLATION_STAGE_016.md) · [name-sequence concordance](I1876_P32_NAME_SEQUENCE_016.md). **Zero medieval manuscript variants verified.**

## B2007 printed page 18 · Direct modern edited-digitized Arabic selected checks

### CR001-001 · revise

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** الأصل الأول في ذكر رتب الذات وتعيّن الأسماء منها والصفات وذكر كيفية انتشاء الفروع من تلك الأسماء والصفات من أصولها مع تعرّض لشرح الأسماء التسعة والتسعين ، وذكر انتشاء باقي الأسماء منها دقيقها وجليلها

**Reviewed English:** First foundation: the ranks of the Essence, the determination of names and attributes from it, the derivation of their branches from their roots, an account of the ninety-nine names, and the derivation of the other names, subtle and great.

**Editorial decision:** The older English said 'differentiation' for تعيّن; preserve the technical 'determination'.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** false. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-002 · annotate

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** بِسْمِ اللَّهِ الرَّحْمنِ الرَّحِيمِ نقل عن رسول اللّه صلّى اللّه عليه وسلّم أنه قال : « قال اللّه عزّ وجلّ : كنت كنزا مخفيّا فأحببت أن أعرف ، فخلقت الخلق لأعرف »

**Reviewed English:** In the name of God, the All-merciful, the Especially Merciful. It is transmitted as from the Messenger of God that God said: 'I was a hidden treasure, and I loved to be known, so I created creation in order to be known.'

**Editorial decision:** Farghānī reports an attribution; the 'hidden treasure' saying lacks an established prophetic isnād. Its interpretive importance must not be treated as authentication.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** false. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-003 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** اعلم أيّدك اللّه بالفهم الصحيح أنه قد كنى في هذا الحديث الإلهي عن كنه الغيب وإطلاق الذات الأقدس وباطن الهوية الأزلية بالكنز المخفي

**Reviewed English:** Know—may God strengthen you with sound understanding—that the hidden treasure in this divine saying figuratively signifies the innermost reality of the Unseen, the nondelimitation of the most holy Essence, and the inward aspect of eternal Identity.

**Editorial decision:** Keep kunh (inner reality) distinct from its iṭlāq (nondelimitation).

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** false. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-004 · revise

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** وذلك لملابسة أن الكنز عبارة عن عين مغيب مكنون وسرّ مخزون مشتمل على جواهر عظيمة الجدوى مرغوب فيها

**Reviewed English:** The analogy arises because a treasure is a concealed and guarded thing, a secret held in reserve, comprising precious jewels whose benefits are great and which are much sought after.

**Editorial decision:** The prior paraphrase omitted the explicit 'great benefit' (ʿaẓīmat al-jadwā) and being sought (marghūb fīhā).

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** false. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-005 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** ما بين أنفس تصان ويضنّ ويستأثر بها وبين نفيس ، ربما يقع فيه التسامح والتبدّل خصوصا أو عموما

**Reviewed English:** Some of these are so precious that they are guarded, withheld and reserved to their possessor; others are precious but may be dealt with more freely or exchanged, privately or generally.

**Editorial decision:** The second comparison is between anfas and nafīs, degrees of relative preciousness.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** false. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-006 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** ومهما أريد إظهاره يعرض أولا عرضا مجملا بحيث يبين الكنز ويظهر لواجده في محله

**Reviewed English:** When it is desired to disclose a treasure, it is first displayed as a whole, so that the treasure becomes evident to its finder in its own place.

**Editorial decision:** Preserve ijmāl (undifferentiated comprehensiveness), not simply a generic presentation.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** false. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-007 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** ثم يعرض ثانيا عرضا يميّز فيه بين الأنفس والنفيس ، وينقل منه عن محله لحكمة يراها الواجد من إظهار كماله الذي يتضمّن غناءه وإظهار التجمّل والجود ونحو ذلك

**Reviewed English:** It is then shown a second time in a display that differentiates what is most precious from what is merely precious; some is removed from its place according to a wisdom perceived by the finder, to display his perfection, including wealth, adornment, generosity and similar qualities.

**Editorial decision:** A figurative sequence of comprehensive and then differentiated disclosure; not a temporal sequence in the divine Essence.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** false. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-008 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** وكذلك كنه الجناب الأقدس تعالى وتقدّس وله المثل الأعلى في السماوات والأرض وهو العزيز الحكيم ، بلا نسبة تشبيه وتشكيل وتمثيل إليه تعالى عن ذلك علوّا كبيرا

**Reviewed English:** Likewise with the innermost reality of the most holy Presence—exalted and sanctified is He, to whom belongs the highest similitude in the heavens and earth, the Mighty and Wise—without attributing to Him any likeness, configuration or representation. He is vastly exalted above that.

**Editorial decision:** The anti-anthropomorphic qualification is part of the authorial analogy, not an editorial gloss.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** false. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-009 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** فإنه الغيب المكنون والسرّ المصون الذي هو أبطن كل باطن

**Reviewed English:** For He is the hidden Unseen and the protected secret, more inward than every inward thing.

**Editorial decision:** This is a divine negative-theological predication in analogy.

### CR001-010 · crux

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** وبطون المشتمل على جواهر أسماء الذات التي هي أنفس نفائس الأسماء

**Reviewed English:** His inwardness contains the jewels of the essential names, the most precious of all names.

**Editorial decision:** The digital text has the awkward sequence بطون المشتمل. Translation conveys contextual sense but a print/manuscript reading is needed before fixing grammar.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** true. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-011 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** التي منها ما يستأثر في مكنون الغيب عنده ، فلا يعلمها إلّا هو ومن ارتفعت بينونته ورفعت كينونته ممن هو أكمل جميع

**Reviewed English:** Among them are names He reserves in the hidden mystery with Him; none knows them except He and one whose separateness and individual selfhood have been lifted away, one of the most perfect among all—

**Editorial decision:** The clause continues across B2007 p.18–19; the wording must not be treated as a finished independent proposition.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

## B2007 printed page 19 · Direct modern edited-digitized Arabic selected checks

### CR001-012 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** الكمّل في عرض التعيّن والتجلّي الأول ، لتحقيق الكمال الذاتي الأشرف المعني بقوله : « فأحببت أن أعرف »

**Reviewed English:** —the perfected, in the presentation of the first determination and first self-disclosure, for realizing the nobler essential perfection intended by 'I loved to be known'.

**Editorial decision:** Continuation from p18; 'in the presentation' preferred to the slightly ambiguous 'participate in the display'.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** false. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-013 · revise

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** وهو المتضمّن أيضا نفائس درر أسماء الصفات التي يسمح بتعريفها لكل من يصلح لتشريفها من كل فرد متخلّق ، أو كامل متحقّق

**Reviewed English:** It also contains the precious jewels of the names of attributes, acquaintance with which is permitted to anyone suited to be honored by them, whether one who has adopted their character or a perfected person who has realized them.

**Editorial decision:** Distinguish takhalluq (assuming qualities) from taḥaqquq (realizing them).

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

### CR001-014 · corrected

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** والمحتوي أيضا على لآلىء أسماء الأفعال العامّ نفعها وأثرها ، والمستفيض حكمها وخبرها في جميع المراتب الكونيّة التي هي محال تمام الكمال الأسمائي والمطلب الغائبّ المعني بقوله : « فخلقت الخلق لأعرف »

**Reviewed English:** It also contains the pearls of the names of acts, whose benefits and effects are universal, and whose judgments and reports extend throughout the cosmic ranks—the loci in which the perfection through the names is completed, the ultimate objective referred to by 'I created creation to be known'.

**Editorial decision:** **Historical-print correction:** I1876 printed p6 (PDF scan 10) reads المطلب الغائي, while B2007 digital reads المطلب الغائبّ. Prefer ‘ultimate objective’ for the edited text; preserve the B2007 form in apparatus. This is a real print-level difference, not a manuscript variant.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** true. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

**Historical 1876 print:** printed p6, source scan 10: **والمطلب الغائي** (historical-print-difference). I1876 p6 visibly reads الغائي where B2007 digital reads الغائبّ. Prefer historic print for a purposive objective; no manuscript inferred.. [Official source viewer](https://sites.dlib.nyu.edu/viewer/books/princeton_aco000264/1?embed=1&lang=en).

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** فإن مفهوم تاء المتكلّم عن نفسه يؤذن بتعيّنه في نفسه وظهوره حالة تكلّمه تعيّنا وظهورا

**Reviewed English:** The first-person tāʾ, by which the speaker speaks of himself, implies his determination in himself and his manifestation when speaking, as determination and manifestation.

### CR001-017 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** أما قوله : « كنزا مخفيّا » ينبئ عن غيبه وإطلاقه بحيث لا يدخل تحت حكم معيّن يحكم عليه من حيث هذا الغيب والإطلاق

**Reviewed English:** By contrast, 'a hidden treasure' signifies His unseen character and nondelimitation, in which no determinate judgment can be applied to Him precisely insofar as He is unseen and undelimited.

### CR001-019 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** وحيث كان الخفاء وقع خبرا « لكنت » عرف سبق الإطلاق والغيب على ذلك التعيّن ، ولكن سبقا رتبيّا لا زمانيّا ، فإنه صحّ أنه ليس عند اللّه صباح ولا مساء

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** وقوله : « فخلقت الخلق لأعرف » يعرف أن تقدير المقدّرات علما ووجودا يعقب ذلك الميل ويترتّب على متعلّقه الذي هو الظهور الحاصل بذلك التعيّن

### CR001-024 · corrected

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** ليتحقّق كمال ذلك الظهور مفصّلا متميّزا ويشمل حكمه وأثره المقدّر والمقدّر تعقّبا ذاتيّا لا زمانيّا ، بل عمليّا ووجوديّا أيضا بالنسبة إلى المقدّر العالم الأزلي وعلمه الذاتي والصفاتي الأبدي

**Reviewed English:** So that the perfection of disclosure may be realized in articulated distinction, and its rule and effect may encompass the determiner and what is determined in essential rather than temporal succession—and also in knowledge and existence with respect to the eternal Determiner who knows, and His everlasting essential and attributive knowledge.

Correct the edited reading to ‘in knowledge and existence’, supported by the immediate wording about the eternal knower and His knowledge. No manuscript readings verified.

Retain historical print reading and existing epistemic/existential sense.. [Official source viewer](https://sites.dlib.nyu.edu/viewer/books/princeton_aco000264/1?embed=1&lang=en).

### CR002-005 · corrected

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** فعلى كل حال يكون ذلك العلم والشهود والتجلّي والإدراك من حيث تعينه ومشيئته وعلمه المقدّس بذاته الأقدس تعالى وتقدّس من حيث واحديتها ، لا من حيث إطلاقها وأحديتها

**Reviewed English:** In every case, this knowing, witnessing, disclosure and apprehension concern His determination, His will and His sacred knowledge of His most holy Essence—exalted and sanctified is He—under the consideration of its oneness (wāḥidiyya), not under that of its nondelimitation and unicity (aḥadiyya).

The corrected B2007 source is confirmed; the phrase also appears in a later quotation in al-Fanārī's Miṣbāḥ al-uns (modern printed text p183), though this is reception testimony not an independent manuscript witness. Direct historical-print check: I1876 p7 (PDF scan 11) also contains من حيث واحديتها. This confirms the correction of the earlier project excerpt; it is not a manuscript variant.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

### CR002-006 · retain

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** فإن إدراكه وشهوده من حيث تعيّنه وعلمه غير ممنوع ، بل موكول إلى مشيئته تعالى وتقدّس كما قال عزّ من قائل : وَلا يُحِيطُونَ بِشَيْءٍ مِنْ عِلْمِهِ إِلَّا بِما شاءَ

**Reviewed English:** For apprehending and witnessing Him under the consideration of His determination and knowledge is not prohibited; it is dependent upon His will, as He says, 'They encompass none of His knowledge except what He wills' (Qurʾān 2:255).

**Editorial decision:** The scope qualifier is essential to avoid treating the Essence's undelimitation as an object of determined knowledge.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** false. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-003-p24-29.js`.

### CR003-009 · revise

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** فكانت هذه القابلية أصل كل قابلية وفاعلية أيضا

**Reviewed English:** This receptivity is the root of every receptivity and also of all activity.

**Editorial decision:** Qābiliyya and fāʿiliyya are correlated active/receptive principial considerations, not created passivity and action.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar

**Editorial decision:** STAGE XV PHOTO UPDATE: I1876 printed p20 scan24 explicitly reads القابل, agreeing with the B2007 digitized Arabic on this disputed word. The earlier conjecture القائل is NOT witnessed in either printed source; retain القابل as the provisional edited lemma. Distinguish receptivity from the seven-name category القائل attested elsewhere. MSS uncollated.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/38?lang=ar

**Source grade:** `modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked`. **I1876 photographic comparison for this clause:** true. **Medieval manuscript compared:** false. **Source dataset:** `data/critical-pass-005-p37-47-p50.js`.

**Historical 1876 print:** printed p20, source scan 24: **والقابل يجمعها** (historic-print-agreement). The noun القابل is present in the photographed 1876 printed page, despite internal parallel mentions of القائل elsewhere. See I1876-L11; no manuscript evidence.. [Official source viewer](https://sites.dlib.nyu.edu/viewer/books/princeton_aco000264/1?embed=1&lang=en) · [exact original photo](https://sites.dlib.nyu.edu/viewer/api/image/books/princeton_aco000264/24/full/2200,/0/default.jpg).

**B2007/derived Arabic selected locus:** كان في عماء ما فوقه هواء ، وما تحته هواء

**Reviewed English:** He was in ʿamāʾ; the following phrase concerning air above and below remains syntactically disputed.

### CR006-015 · corrected

**B2007 digital Arabic selected locus (diplomatic):** تفرّع ( سم الحكيم ) منه

**Provisional photographic 1876 edited Arabic:** تفرّع ( اسم الحكم ) منه

**Reviewed English:** From it branches the name **the Judge** (*al-Ḥakam*).

**Editorial decision:** STAGE XVI fully photographed I1876 printed p32, image scan36 (2200×3370px), positively reads **اسم الحكم**; the modern Beirut digital text reads **سم الحكيم**. These represent different names, al-Ḥakam and al-Ḥakīm, plus an omitted initial alif in digital `سم`. Prefer the older photographed reading for the working edited text, but **do not** call this a manuscript variant or an error of the physical Beirut edition until actual B2007 paper and independent manuscripts are examined. The same al-Ḥakam is discussed again under Sovereignty at p59, in a different `iʿtibār`.

**Source URL:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/52?lang=ar

**Photographed I1876:** [printed page 32, original-resolution scan 36](https://github.com/gnamma-research/booksite/blob/master/texts/al-farghani/muntaha-al-madarik/facsimile-I1876-hires/images/scan_0036.jpg), source image SHA256 `8a5f2a25fb93cc6560442dd7c0b9f8e9499f833df7b5c33153e12ea4c0463950`.

## Full precritical English and required next work

The complete 128-page-labelled English working translation remains in [WORKING_TRANSLATION_18_145.md](WORKING_TRANSLATION_18_145.md); the >1.8 MB older cumulative dossier is archived separately as [FULL_CUMULATIVE_CRITICAL_007.md](FULL_CUMULATIVE_CRITICAL_007.md). Neither is a final critically established text.

**Next:** photograph actual B2007 pp38,48,52,61–62; inspect original-resolution 1876 print p32 for the possible `اسم الحكم`/ `اسم الحكيم` discrepancy; then obtain real folios of C1107, R670, K273, F3966, Y706 and run systematic full-page and manuscript collation, with individual line/folio concordances. The next major work is *not* additional scattered excerpts.
